Use quando o usuario escolher "1" no menu de modos do CLAUDE.md ou se identificar como nao-desenvolvedor. ([Joao-19/CasaShopping-guide](https://github.com/Joao-19/CasaShopping-guide/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/modo-vibecode) · ★3)

The scaffolded mod has a switch for every function, an opt-in debug log that masks secrets, and no MCP server.
